- **spec**: A command MAY declare the arguments it accepts and MAY answer with a result. What it declares SHALL be discoverable without running it: each argument SHALL carry a name, the kind of value it takes, whether it is required, and a description of what it means. ([yesbert/loomweaver](https://github.com/yesbert/loomweaver/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-08-26-commands-are-callable-tools/specs/commands/spec.md) · ★8)
- **spec**: A command MAY declare a keyboard shortcut in a platform-neutral form, and the workbench SHALL bind and display it in the spelling of the platform it is running on. Bindings SHALL follow the registered commands, so a shortcut appears and disappears with the plugin that owns it. ([yesbert/loomweaver](https://github.com/yesbert/loomweaver/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-10-12-a-shortcut-hint-names-keys-in-the-interface-language/specs/commands/spec.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **spec**: A command SHALL be able to state what running it on an agent's word alone amounts to, as one of four statements: that the agent's word is enough; that the person is asked first; that the person is asked every time it is called; or that it is not to be run on an agent's word at all. A command that states nothing states nothing, which is the default and means what the platform does today. ([yesbert/loomweaver](https://github.com/yesbert/loomweaver/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-09-17-a-command-says-what-an-agents-word-is-enough-for/specs/commands/spec.md) · ★8)
- **spec**: A plugin SHALL register a command once, and any control MAY point at it by its identity instead of carrying its own implementation. Where a control names a command, that command SHALL be what runs. A control MAY still carry an inline implementation, and where it does both, the named command wins. ([yesbert/loomweaver](https://github.com/yesbert/loomweaver/blob/HEAD/openspec/specs/commands/spec.md) · ★8)
- **spec**: The connection that runs the workbench's commands for an agent SHALL follow one run at a time. When a run starts, it SHALL forget every call an earlier run left open, so that such a call is neither run nor answered later. Where a plugin checks a call before it runs and that check fails or gives no decision, the call SHALL be refused and its command SHALL NOT run. The agent SHALL be told only that ([yesbert/loomweaver](https://github.com/yesbert/loomweaver/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-11-06-the-review-before-the-next-release/specs/commands/spec.md) · ★8)
- **spec**: A distribution SHALL be able to place a visible entry point to the search over open work in its chrome, showing the shortcut that also opens it, and sized to match its neighbours in the bar it sits in. It SHALL be placeable independently of the entry point to the command search, so that a product may offer either, both, or neither, and may put each in a different bar. ([yesbert/loomweaver](https://github.com/yesbert/loomweaver/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-08-28-the-workbench-shows-its-shortcuts/specs/commands/spec.md) · ★8)
- **spec**: The connection that runs the workbench's commands for an agent SHALL run a call only where the command it names is among the tools it offers at that moment. A call that names anything else SHALL be answered as refused, saying why, and SHALL NOT run, even where the plugin holding the connection could run that command itself. A command its plugin did not open to callers is therefore beyond an agent' ([yesbert/loomweaver](https://github.com/yesbert/loomweaver/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-10-23-the-audit-before-0-20-0/specs/commands/spec.md) · ★8)
- **spec**: The workbench SHALL offer a second search over open work, distinct from the command search and reached by its own gesture. It SHALL list what is open, most recently active first, and choosing one SHALL reveal it where it is. It SHALL offer the actions of a piece of open work without leaving the search. ([yesbert/loomweaver](https://github.com/yesbert/loomweaver/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-09-25-defects-found-while-reading-are-fixed/specs/commands/spec.md) · ★8)
- **spec**: The connection that runs the workbench's commands for an agent SHALL offer a policy a product may choose to apply before each call, acting on what the called command states about an agent's word. A product that does not apply it SHALL be asked nothing and SHALL have nothing decided for it, as before. ([yesbert/loomweaver](https://github.com/yesbert/loomweaver/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-10-06-an-agent-connection-can-ask-for-the-product/specs/commands/spec.md) · ★8)
- **spec**: Where the workbench offers more than one command in the same list, no two of the commands it ships SHALL present the same name in any language it ships. A name SHALL say what its own command does, so that two commands whose effects differ do not differ only by a word one of them leaves out. ([yesbert/loomweaver](https://github.com/yesbert/loomweaver/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-08-30-each-reset-names-what-it-resets/specs/commands/spec.md) · ★8)
- **spec**: Every trigger SHALL run its command through one place, so that access rules, the rule about a window showing a single piece of work and failure reporting cannot differ by route. A command that fails SHALL be reported rather than leaving an unhandled failure, and a command refused for lack of permission SHALL surface to the user rather than only to the console. ([yesbert/loomweaver](https://github.com/yesbert/loomweaver/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-08-26-the-record-is-of-what-the-user-picked/specs/commands/spec.md) · ★8)

- **spec-driven-development**: 仕様駆動開発プロセスを起動するスキル。要件→設計→実装計画→実装の4フェーズを、 エージェント協調と3段階エスカレーション（Phase 1/2/3）で進行させる。 以下のいずれかに該当する場合は必ずこのスキルを使用する。 - `/spec-driven-development [タスク]` の明示的呼び出しがあった - 新規機能・システムを仕様策定から実装完了まで体系的に進めたい - 「誰が書いても同じ実装になる」レベルの仕様精度を全フェーズで確保したい - 複数の専門領域（基本設計・セキュリティ・アーキテクチャ・TDD・Git・ コードレビュー）を協調させて実装を進めたい - エージェント間の判断対立や判断不能を体系的にエスカレーションしたい - 仕様の決定背景・代替案・トレードオフを文書として残したい - 実装中の曖昧性を排除し、再現可能な開発プロセスで進行させたい 各フェーズには時間制約（§0.5）、リスク分類基準（§0.3）、対立解消手順（§5）が 定義されており、エスカレーション発生時は §0.6 ログスキーマに記録される。 単発の実装タスクや既存コードの軽微な修正には使用しない。 ([YSHK721/GetAccelerometerData](https://github.com/YSHK721/GetAccelerometerData/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/spec-driven-development.md))
- **spec-driven-development**: Creates specs before coding. Use when starting a new project, feature, or significant change and no specification exists yet.
